- **eu-accessibility**: Turn "make it accessible" into numbered, testable acceptance criteria. This is the only regime in the repo that is fully testable — use that. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-accessibility.md))
- **eu-ai-act**: [COMMUNITY] Assess EU AI Act (Regulation 2024/1689) compliance obligations, risk classification, and conformity requirements for AI systems ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-ai-act.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **eu-ai-act**: Classify in this order — **prohibited, then role, then tier, then duties**. A team that starts at "what documentation do we need" has skipped the two questions that could have ended the project or moved the obligations onto someone else. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-ai-act.md))
- **eu-ai-act-review**: EU AI Act review. First-pass triage of where your AI product sits under the EU AI Act. Not legal advice. (UX+AI, Design for AI) ([IleanaMarcut/uxai-mcp-plugin](https://github.com/IleanaMarcut/uxai-mcp-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/uxai/commands/eu-ai-act-review.md) · ★2)
- **eu-ai-act-specialist**: EU AI Act (Regulation (EU) 2024/1689) operational compliance for compliance teams. Three Article-level decisions: (1) What's the risk tier of this AI system — prohibited (Art. 5), high-risk (Art. 6 + Annex III), limited-risk (Art. 50), or minimal-risk? (2) For high-risk systems, what's the Article ([ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine](https://github.com/ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/eu-ai-act-specialist.md))
- **eu-applicability**: From the facts in `compliance/compliance.yaml`, decide what applies — and, more importantly, record what does not, with the fact that decides it and the condition that would revive it. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-applicability.md))
- **eu-cloud-sovereignty**: [COMMUNITY] Assess EU Cloud Sovereignty Framework (v1.2.1) posture for cloud procurement — score the eight sovereignty objectives and record SEAL-level evidence ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-cloud-sovereignty.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **eu-compliance-gates**: Everything else produces a record of what is true today. This wires it into CI so the code cannot drift away from it. A dependency is added, a field appears, the 50th person is hired, a due date passes — none of it announces itself, and by the time anyone notices, the brief describes a product that has not existed for a year. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-compliance-gates.md))
- **eu-consent-and-tracking**: The most commonly broken rule in European software, and the cheapest to fix before launch. Consent is required before **storing or reading anything on a user's device** unless it is strictly necessary for a service the user explicitly requested — and that is far broader than cookies. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-consent-and-tracking.md))
- **eu-cra**: [COMMUNITY] Assess EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA, Regulation 2024/2847) compliance obligations for products with digital elements placed on the EU market ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-cra.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **eu-cra-secure-by-design**: Cybersecurity as a market-access condition. Scope first, because the answer is counterintuitive: **pure SaaS is generally out; monetised open source is generally in.** ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-cra-secure-by-design.md))
- **eu-data-act**: [COMMUNITY] Assess EU Data Act (Regulation 2023/2854) compliance for connected products, data holders, and data processing service providers ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-data-act.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **eu-dora**: [COMMUNITY] Assess DORA (Digital Operational Resilience Act, EU 2022/2554) compliance for financial sector entities operating in the EU ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-dora.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **eu-dsa**: [COMMUNITY] Assess EU Digital Services Act (DSA, Regulation 2022/2065) compliance obligations for online intermediary services, platforms, and very large online platforms ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-dsa.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **eu-dsa-platform**: The DSA is layered and the layer decides the duty list. The costly mistake is concluding "the DSA is for Big Tech": **hosting duties have no size exemption.** If one user can publish something another user sees, you owe Arts. 16-18 whatever your headcount. The micro/small exemption removes **Section 3 only**. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-dsa-platform.md))
- **eu-evidence-pack**: Assemble what a regulator, auditor or acquirer actually asks for. The property that matters: **evidence has to exist before you need it.** Every document here is trivial to produce while building and nearly impossible to reconstruct honestly eighteen months later. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-evidence-pack.md))
- **eu-gdpr-data-map**: Build the table everything else hangs off: *field → purpose → lawful basis → retention → deletion path → recipients*, from the schema and the code, not from the privacy policy. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-gdpr-data-map.md))
- **eu-grill-me**: Before writing the thing the user asked for, find out what it actually is in the detail regulation turns on, and **do not build the gated parts** until the deciding facts are recorded or explicitly, attributably accepted as unknown. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-grill-me.md))
- **eu-incident-response**: One event, several regulators, different deadlines from different trigger events. The failure is not ignorance of the rules; it is that at 02:00 on a Saturday nobody remembers which clock started when, and the 24-hour ones are gone before triage finishes. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-incident-response.md))
- **eu-nis2**: [COMMUNITY] Assess NIS2 Directive compliance obligations for EU member state operators of essential services and important entities ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-nis2.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
- **eu-nis2-readiness**: NIS2 regulates the **organisation**, not the product — which is why teams who scope their software carefully miss it entirely. A large number of ordinary SaaS companies are "cloud computing service providers" under Annex I and do not know it. And it is a **Directive**: the binding text is the national transposition. ([ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills](https://github.com/ArttuAn/eu-compliance-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/eu-nis2-readiness.md))
- **eu-rgpd**: [COMMUNITY] Generate GDPR (EU 2016/679) compliance assessment for EU/EEA data processing — legal basis mapping, data subject rights, transfers, DPIA screening, and breach notification across all member states ([tractorjuice/arc-kit](https://github.com/tractorjuice/arc-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/arckit-claude/plugins/eu/commands/eu-rgpd.md) · ★2.2k · 3 copies)
